Constraints\n- None currently specified (possible enhancement: `{name:alpha}` for filters)\n\n## Navigation Links Structure\n\n### Home Page Links\n- Teams: `/tim` (custom route)\n- Tournaments: Use it for:\n- Creating new list/index views (`.cshtml`)\n- Implementing entity card/grid layouts\n- Adding filtering and sorting\n- Implementing pagination\n- Creating breadcrumb navigation\n- Adding links to detail pages\n\n## When to Use This Skill\n- "Create a list page for Coaches"\n- "Add a new index view that displays all Sponsors"\n- "Build a list page with cards for Players"\n- "Create a searchable list of Teams"\n- "Add pagination to the Venues list"\n- "Make a grid display for Tournaments with filters"\n\n## Workflow\n\n### 1. Create Controller Action\nAdd an `Index` action in the controller that:\n- Fetches all entities from DbContext\n- Maps to ViewModels if needed\n- Passes to the view\n\n```csharp\n[Route("")]\n[Route("pregled")]\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Index()\n{\n var entities = await _dbContext.EntityName\n .AsNoTracking()\n .ToListAsync();\n \n var viewModel = entities\n .Select(e => new EntityCardViewModel(\n title: e.Name,\n subtitle: e.Subtitle ?? "N/A",\n body: e.Description ?? "",\n linkText: "View Details",\n linkUrl: Url.Action("Details", new { id = e.Id })\n ))\n .ToList();\n \n return View(viewModel);\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Create the Index View\nLocation: `Views/{ControllerName}/Index.cshtml`\n\nBase structure:\n```html\n@model IEnumerable<EntityCardViewModel>\n\n@{\n ViewData["Title"] = "Entity List";\n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("Entities")\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n}\n\n<div class="container mt-4">\n <h1>@ViewData["Title"]</h1>\n \n <div class="row">\n @foreach (var item in Model)\n {\n <div class="col-md-4 mb-3">\n <partial name="Components/_Card" model="item" />\n </div>\n }\n </div>\n</div>\n```\n\n### 3. Use the Card Component\nThe `_Card.cshtml` partial is already available in `Views/Shared/Components/_Card.cshtml`.\nIt accepts `EntityCardViewModel` and renders a Bootstrap card.\n\n```html\n<partial name="Components/_Card" model="viewModelItem" />\n```\n\n### 4. (Optional) Add Filtering/Search\n```csharp\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Index(string search)\n{\n var query = _dbContext.EntityName.AsQueryable();\n \n if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))\n {\n query = query.Where(e => e.Name.Contains(search) || e.Description.Contains(search));\n }\n \n var entities = await query.ToListAsync();\n // ... rest of action\n}\n```\n\nView form:\n```html\n<form method="get" class="mb-3">\n <div class="input-group">\n <input type="text" name="search" class="form-control" placeholder="Search..." value="@Context.Request.Query["search"]">\n <button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit">Search</button>\n </div>\n</form>\n```\n\n### 5. (Optional) Add Pagination\nUse `PagedList` NuGet package or simple offset/limit pattern:\n\n```csharp\nconst int pageSize = 12;\nint page = int.TryParse(pageRequest, out int p) ? p : 1;\nvar total = await query.CountAsync();\nvar items = await query\n .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize)\n .Take(pageSize)\n .ToListAsync();\n\n// Pass paging info to view\nViewData["CurrentPage"] = page;\nViewData["TotalPages"] = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)total / pageSize);\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **ViewModels**: Always map entities to ViewModels for cleaner views\n - Use `EntityCardViewModel` for card layouts\n - Include only necessary fields for display\n\n2. **Breadcrumbs**: Always add breadcrumb navigation\n ```csharp\n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("Entities")\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n ```\n\n3. **Async/Await**: Always use async database calls\n - `await _dbContext.Entity.ToListAsync()`\n - `AsNoTracking()` for read-only operations\n\n4. **Naming**: \n - View file: `Views/{Controller}/Index.cshtml`\n - Route: `[Route("")]` and `[Route("pregled")]` for list views\n\n5. **Responsive Design**:\n - Use Bootstrap grid: `col-md-4 col-lg-3` for card layouts\n - Ensure mobile-friendly display\n\n6. **Empty State**: Handle when no entities exist\n ```html\n @if (Model.Count() == 0)\n {\n <div class="alert alert-info">\n No entities found.\n </div>\n }\n ```\n\n## Common Tasks\n\n### Display All Entities as Cards\n1. Create controller action with entity list\n2. Map to `EntityCardViewModel` collection\n3. Create `Index.cshtml` with card grid\n4. Use `_Card` partial in loop\n5. Set breadcrumbs in `ViewData["Breadcrumbs"]`\n\n### Add Search/Filter\n1. Add `search` parameter to Index action\n2. Filter DbSet based on parameter\n3. Add form with search box above grid\n4. Preserve search in query string\n\n### Add Pagination\n1. Calculate total count and pages\n2. Use `Skip()` and `Take()` on query\n3. Pass page info via ViewData\n4. Add pagination controls below grid\n\n## Example: Complete List Page for Coaches\n\n**Controller Action**:\n```csharp\n[Route("treneri")]\npublic class CoachesController : Controller\n{\n private readonly TournamentDbContext _dbContext;\n \n [Route("")]\n [Route("pregled")]\n public async Task<IActionResult> Index()\n {\n var coaches = await _dbContext.Coaches\n .AsNoTracking()\n .ToListAsync();\n \n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("Coaches")\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n \n var viewModel = coaches\n .Select(c => new EntityCardViewModel(\n title: c.Name,\n subtitle: c.GamerTag,\n body: $"Experience: {c.YearsOfExperience} years\\nHired: {c.HiredAt:yyyy-MM-dd}",\n linkText: "View Profile",\n linkUrl: Url.Action("Details", new { id = c.Id })\n ))\n .ToList();\n \n return View(viewModel);\n }\n}\n```\n\n**View** (`Views/Coaches/Index.cshtml`):\n```html\n@model IEnumerable<EntityCardViewModel>\n\n@{\n ViewData["Title"] = "Coaches";\n}\n\n<div class="container mt-4">\n <h1>@ViewData["Title"]</h1>\n \n <div class="row">\n @foreach (var coach in Model)\n {\n <div class="col-md-4 mb-3">\n <partial name="Components/_Card" model="coach" />\n </div>\n }\n </div>\n</div>\n```\n\n## File Locations\n- Entity models: `Models/{EntityName}.cs`\n- Views: `Views/{ControllerName}/Index.cshtml`\n- View models: `ViewModels/{ViewModelName}.cs`\n- Components: `Views/Shared/Components/`\n,filePath:d:\\ASP .NET ALL\\2025-2026\\League of Legends Tournament Hosting\\.github\\skills\\list-page-skill.md},tool_use_id:toolu_bdrk_011XkKWgC6w1siohPnKkSCLE__vscode-1777943072056,cwd:d:\\ASP .NET ALL\\2025-2026\\League of Legends Tournament Hosting}

Use it for:\n- Creating Edit/Create views (`.cshtml` forms)\n- Building form layouts with validation\n- Implementing POST handlers\n- Adding dropdown/select lists for foreign keys\n- Client-side validation\n- Server-side validation messages\n\n## When to Use This Skill\n- "Create an edit form for Coach model"\n- "Add a form to create new Players"\n- "Build a form to edit Tournament details"\n- "Create a Sponsor registration form"\n- "Add validation to Team edit form"\n- "Create a form with dropdown for selecting Coach and Manager"\n\n## Important Note\n**This project follows the Index + Details pattern only (no Edit/Create pages in current design).** This skill is for:\n1. **Future extensions** when Edit/Create functionality is needed\n2. **Reference implementation** for how forms should be structured\n3. **Optional feature**: You can extend the app with Edit/Create as enhancement\n\n## Workflow\n\n### 1. Create Controller Actions\n\n**GET action** (display empty/prefilled form):\n```csharp\n[Route("uredi/{id:int}")]\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id)\n{\n var entity = await _dbContext.EntityName.FindAsync(id);\n if (entity == null) return NotFound();\n \n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("Entities", Url.Action("Index")),\n new($"Edit {entity.Name}")\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n \n // For dropdowns: Load related entities\n ViewData["CoachOptions"] = await _dbContext.Coaches.ToListAsync();\n \n return View(entity);\n}\n```\n\n**POST action** (handle form submission):\n```csharp\n[HttpPost]\n[Route("uredi/{id:int}")]\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, EntityName model)\n{\n if (id != model.Id) return BadRequest();\n \n if (!ModelState.IsValid)\n {\n ViewData["CoachOptions"] = await _dbContext.Coaches.ToListAsync();\n return View(model);\n }\n \n try\n {\n _dbContext.Update(model);\n await _dbContext.SaveChangesAsync();\n return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id = model.Id });\n }\n catch (DbUpdateException)\n {\n ModelState.AddModelError("", "Unable to save changes. Database error.");\n return View(model);\n }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Create the Edit Form View\nLocation: `Views/{ControllerName}/Edit.cshtml`\n\nBase structure:\n```html\n@model EntityName\n\n@{\n ViewData["Title"] = "Edit Entity";\n}\n\n<div class="container mt-4">\n <h1>@ViewData["Title"]</h1>\n \n <form method="post" class="form">\n <input type="hidden" asp-for="Id" />\n \n <div class="form-group mb-3">\n <label asp-for="Name" class="form-label">Name</label>\n <input type="text" asp-for="Name" class="form-control" required />\n <span asp-validation-for="Name" class="text-danger"></span>\n </div>\n \n <div class="form-group mb-3">\n <label asp-for="CoachId" class="form-label">Coach</label>\n <select asp-for="CoachId" asp-items="ViewData["CoachOptions"] as SelectList" class="form-control">\n <option value="">-- Select Coach --</option>\n </select>\n <span asp-validation-for="CoachId" class="text-danger"></span>\n </div>\n \n <div class="form-group">\n <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">Save</button>\n <a href="@Url.Action("Details", new { id = Model.Id })" class="btn btn-secondary">Cancel</a>\n </div>\n </form>\n</div>\n\n@section Scripts {\n @await Html.PartialAsync("_ValidationScriptsPartial")\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Create a Create Form\nSimilar to Edit, but POST to different action:\n\n**Controller**:\n```csharp\n[Route("novi")]\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Create()\n{\n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("Entities", Url.Action("Index")),\n new("Create New")\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n ViewData["CoachOptions"] = await ViewData["CoachOptions"] = await _dbContext.Coaches.ToListAsync();\n return View(model);\n }\n \n _dbContext.Add(model);\n await _dbContext.SaveChangesAsync();\n \n return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id = model.Id });\n}\n```\n\n**View** (`Views/Entity/Create.cshtml`):\n```html\n@model EntityName\n\n@{\n ViewData["Title"] = "Create New Entity";\n}\n\n<div {\n @await Html.PartialAsync("_ValidationScriptsPartial")\n}\n```\n\n## Form Input Types\n\n### Text Input\n```html\n<input type="text" asp-for="PropertyName" class="form-control" />\n```\n\n### TextArea\n```html\n<textarea asp-for="PropertyName" class="form-control" rows="4"></textarea>\n```\n\n### Dropdown/Select\n```html\n<select asp-for="PropertyName" asp-items="@ViewData["Options"] as SelectList" class="form-control">\n <option value="">-- Select --</option>\n</select>\n```\n\nIn controller:\n```csharp\nViewData["Options"] = new SelectList(await _dbContext.Coaches.ToListAsync(), "Id", "Name");\n```\n\n### Date Input\n```html\n<input type="date" asp-for="HiredAt" class="form-control" />\n```\n\n### Number Input\n```html\n<input type="number" asp-for="YearsOfExperience" class="form-control" min="0" />\n```\n\n### Checkbox\n```html\n<input type="checkbox" asp-for="IsActive" class="form-check-input" />\n<label asp-for="IsActive" class="form-check-label">Is Active</label>\n```\n\n## Validation\n\n### Client-Side Validation\nAlways include at end of form view:\n```html\n@section Scripts {\n @await Html.PartialAsync("_ValidationScriptsPartial")\n}\n```\n\n### Server-Side Validation\nIn controller action:\n```csharp\nif (!ModelState.IsValid)\n{\n // Return view with errors shown\n return View(model);\n}\n```\n\nDisplay validation errors:\n```html\n<span asp-validation-for="PropertyName" class="text-danger"></span>\n```\n\n### Data Annotations for Validation\nIn model:\n```csharp\n[Required(ErrorMessage = "Name is required")]\n[StringLength(100, MinimumLength = 3)]\npublic string Name { get; set; }\n\n[Range(1, 50, ErrorMessage = "Experience must be 1-50 years")]\npublic int YearsOfExperience { get; set; }\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always Include Hidden Id Field** (for Edit):\n ```html\n <input type="hidden" asp-for="Id" />\n ```\n\n2. **Breadcrumbs**: Always add to forms\n ```csharp\n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("List", Url.Action("Index")),\n new("Edit")\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n ```\n\n3. **Load Related Entities**: For foreign keys\n ```csharp\n ViewData["CoachOptions"] = await _dbContext.Coaches.ToListAsync();\n ```\n\n4. **Responsive Layout**: Use Bootstrap new { id = Model.Id })" class="btn btn-secondary">Cancel</a>\n ```\n\n6. **Error Messages**: Display custom messages\n ```html\n @if (!ModelState.IsValid)\n {\n <div class="alert alert-danger">\n Please correct the errors below.\n </div>\n }\n ```\n\n7. **Async/Await**: Always use async database calls\n ```csharp\n var entity = await _dbContext.Entity.FindAsync(id);\n ```\n\n## Common It handles the full workflow of adding form views, controller actions, validation, and submission handling.\n\n## When to Use This Skill\n- "Create a new Create form for Players"\n- "Add an Edit page for Teams"\n- "Implement form validation for Sponsor registration"\n- "Add POST handler for form submission"\n- "Create a form with dropdown lists for foreign keys"\n- "Implement client-side and server-side validation"\n\n## Workflow\n\n### 1. Create Controller Actions\n\n**GET Action** (Display the form):\n```csharp\n[HttpGet]\n[Route("create")] // or [Route("edit/{id:int}")]\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Create() // or Edit(int id)\n{\n // For edit: load existing entity\n // var entity = await _dbContext.Entity.FindAsync(id);\n \n // Load data for dropdowns\n ViewData["RelatedEntities"] = await _dbContext.RelatedEntities.ToListAsync();\n \n // Set breadcrumbs\n var breadcrumbs = new List<BreadcrumbItem>\n {\n new("Home", "/"),\n new("Entities", Url.Action("Index")),\n new("Create Entity") // or "Edit Entity"\n };\n ViewData["Breadcrumbs"] = breadcrumbs;\n \n return View(/* entity or new Entity() */);\n}\n```\n\n**POST Action** (Handle form submission):\n```csharp\n[HttpPost]\n[Route("create")]\n[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]\npublic async Task<IActionResult> Create(Entity model)\n{\n if (!ModelState.IsValid)\n {\n // Reload dropdown data\n ViewData["RelatedEntities"] = await _dbContext.RelatedEntities.ToListAsync();\n return View(model);\n }\n \n try\n {\n _dbContext.Add(model);\n await _dbContext.SaveChangesAsync();\n return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));\n }\n catch (Exception)\n {\n ModelState.AddModelError("", "Unable to save changes.");\n return View(model);\n }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. It covers modifying models, configuring relationships, generating migrations, and managing database schema changes.\n\n## When to Use This Skill\n- "Add a new property to the Player model"\n- "Create a many-to-many relationship between Teams and Tournaments"\n- "Generate a migration for model changes"\n- "Configure owned types for embedded data"\n- "Fix a foreign key constraint"\n- "Update DbContext for new entities"\n\n## Workflow\n\n### 1. Modify Entity Models\n\nUpdate the model class in `Models/{Entity}.cs`:\n```csharp\npublic class Entity\n{\n [Key]\n public int Id { get; set; }\n \n [Required]\n [StringLength(100)]\n public string Name { get; set; }\n \n // Add new property\n public string NewProperty { get; set; }\n \n // Navigation property\n public virtual RelatedEntity RelatedEntity { get; set; }\n \n // Foreign key\n public int RelatedEntityId { get; set; }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Configure Relationships in DbContext\n\nModify `Data/TournamentDbContext.cs`:\n```csharp\nprotected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)\n{\n // One-to-Many\n modelBuilder.Entity<Entity>()\n .HasOne(e => e.RelatedEntity)\n .WithMany(r => r.Entities)\n .HasForeignKey(e => e.RelatedEntityId);\n \n // Many-to-Many\n modelBuilder.Entity<Entity>()\n .HasMany(e => e.RelatedEntities)\n .WithMany(r => r.Entities)\n .UsingEntity(j => j.ToTable("EntityRelatedEntities"));\n \n // Owned type\n modelBuilder.Entity<Entity>().OwnsOne(e => e.OwnedData);\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Generate Migration\n\nRun in terminal:\n```powershell\ndotnet ef migrations add MigrationName --context TournamentDbContext\n```\n\n### 4. Apply Migration\n\n```powershell\ndotnet ef database update --context TournamentDbContext\n```\n\n### 5.
